## Best date plans

### 2026-10-01 — Little Egg + Lambchop

**Dinner, then Lambchop.** American breakfast and brunch at Little Egg. It is 1.2 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Lambchop starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=657+Washington+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&destination=233+Butler+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://publicrecords.nyc/) · [Event booking](https://www.songkick.com/concerts/43200747-lambchop-at-public-records) · [Restaurant](https://www.littleeggbk.com/)

**Dinner, then Yo-Yo Ma with Gustavo Dudamel.** Mexican at Casa Enrique. It is 2.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Yo-Yo Ma with Gustavo Dudamel starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.

### 2026-09-28 — NYC Resistor Public Craft Night + Gage & Tollner

**NYC Resistor Public Craft Night, then dinner.** Oyster and chop house at Gage & Tollner. It is a 12-minute walk from the venue. It runs to 9:30 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.

#### [Shepard Fairey: Untitled (Art Car)](https://cartdept.com/feed/cart-department-presents-shepard-fairey-art-car)

12:00 PM · unusual · Free · Free Parking Gallery · West Village · Manhattan · Score 73.0  
Shepard Fairey transforms a 1937 Packard Touring Sedan into a metallic-gold art car connecting street art, punk, hip-hop, graphic design, and automobile culture.
**Timing:** Final day; check CART Department for gallery hours.
**Practical note:** Indoor gallery stop works as a storm-day alternative; confirm hours before leaving.
**Why now:** The art-car presentation is on view September 24 through September 27 only.
**Book soon:** Final day of the four-day presentation.
[Official](https://cartdept.com/feed/cart-department-presents-shepard-fairey-art-car) · [Discovered via The Skint](https://www.theskint.com/ongoing-events/)

8:00 AM · outdoors · Free · Bryant Park · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 45.4  
Urban naturalist Gabriel Willow leads a free fall-migration birding walk in Bryant Park, looking for warblers, tanagers, vireos, thrushes, and other migrants.
**Timing:** Tours are Tuesdays at 8am during migration season; meet at the Bryant Park info kiosk.
**Practical note:** No pre-registration required; bring binoculars if you have them.
**Why now:** Tuesday morning fall-migration tour in Bryant Park.

#### [The Amazing Maize Maze](https://www.queensfarm.org/event/amazing-maize-maze-24/)

11:00 AM · outdoors · $17 adults; $10 children ages 3-11; free ages 2 and under · Queens County Farm Museum · Floral Park · Queens · Score 47.0  
NYC's only corn maze sends visitors through a three-acre labyrinth with a Stalk Talk, clues, puzzles, and a Victory Bridge overlook.
**Timing:** Last entry 4pm; Saturdays and Sundays through November 1.
**Practical note:** Outdoor maze; Saturday forecast is cooler and partly sunny, but wear shoes for uneven farm ground.
**Why now:** The maze is open weekends in the fall and active on October 3.
**On-sale watch:** Timed farm attractions can sell through busy fall weekends.

## Where to eat

Official menu captures and Eater/Myrtle Avenue event evidence support a mix of current restaurant cards, verified set menus, finite cocktail offers, and recent openings; older heatmap entries are retained only as restaurants, not September-new openings.

Dining research: 2026-09-27T10:30:16+00:00

No NYC Michelin-star claims were verified for these candidates. Eater’s Manhattan heatmap says the Mexico City original El Califa de León was the world’s first Michelin-starred taqueria in 2024; that should not be copied as an award for the New York location. Michelin Guide pages were not separately fetched, so unsourced award claims should be omitted.

### Lunch prix-fixe noted on Crown Shy's official menu

Special menus — Crown Shy

Official menu page lists a lunch menu and explicitly notes that a prix-fixe menu is available, making it a useful weekday downtown set-menu lead.

Price: prix-fixe price unknown; a la carte lunch items shown include chicken kofta $27, salmon $29, pork katsu sandwich $29  
Schedule: Lunch; exact lunch days/hours not stated on the menu page checked  
Conditions: Official Crown Shy menu says “Prix Fixe Menu Available,” but the prix-fixe price, courses, tax, tip, and service charge were not visible in the captured menu text; à la carte lunch prices are verified from https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/.  
[Source](https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/)

### Junoon lunch prestige prix-fixe in Flatiron

Special menus — Junoon

Official menu lists Junoon’s lunch Prestige prix-fixe with appetizer, main, and dessert choices; mains are served with basmati rice and naan.

Price: 2-course Prestige lunch $43; 3-course Prestige lunch $49; accompaniments shown $6-$18  
Schedule: Open Tuesday-Sunday per official page metadata; lunch service hours not stated in fetched menu text  
Conditions: Official menu states a 20% service charge will be added to parties of 5 or more; tax/tip beyond that and lunch hours were not stated in captured text. Source: https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/.  
[Source](https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/)

### Le Rock lunch at Rockefeller Center

More restaurants — Le Rock

Official page shows Le Rock serving a weekday lunch menu with brasserie staples such as moules frites, cheeseburger deluxe, lobster roll, and fruits de mer, useful for Midtown dining before museums/theater.

Price: examples: moules frites $32, cheeseburger deluxe $32, lobster roll $44, steak frites $60  
Schedule: Official structured data lists Mon-Fri 11:00-21:00 and Sat 16:00-21:00; menu page metadata says Mon-Fri 5:30 PM-10:30 PM, so lunch availability should be reconfirmed  
Conditions: Ordinary official lunch menu, not a discount or special. Captured restaurant schema lists opening hours Mon-Fri 11:00-21:00 and Sat 16:00-21:00; tax/tip/service charges not stated. Source: https://www.lerocknyc.com/menus/.  
[Source](https://www.lerocknyc.com/menus/)

### Manhatta bar food with skyline views

More restaurants — Manhatta

Official menu lists a bar food menu with snacks, oysters, skewers, lobster roll, burger, and cocktails, useful as a less formal alternative to the dining room at a high-view downtown restaurant.

Price: examples: olives $9, hand-cut chips $12, lobster roll $22, dry-aged burger $29, oysters $25 half-dozen  
Schedule: Bar schedule not stated in fetched menu text  
Conditions: Ordinary bar/restaurant menu, not a happy hour or discount; captured menu also showed separate prix-fixe dining-room menus. Bar schedule, entry policy, tax, tip, and service charge were not stated in captured menu text. Source: https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/menus/.  
[Source](https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/menus/)

### Greywind weekday lunch near Hudson Yards

More restaurants — Greywind / Bakery at Greywind

Official menu lists weekday lunch sandwiches and salads, a practical Midtown West/Hudson Yards option from Dan Kluger's group after Loring Place's closure redirected diners to Greywind/Spygold.

Price: weekday lunch sandwiches $16-$18; separate Lunch Prix Fixe listed at $35 per person Monday-Friday 11:30am-2:30pm  
Schedule: Lunch Mon-Fri, 11am-3pm per official menu description  
Conditions: Ordinary lunch restaurant card; captured menu separately lists a $35 weekday lunch prix-fixe. Tax/tip/service charges not stated. Source: https://www.greywindnyc.com/menus/.  
[Source](https://www.greywindnyc.com/menus/)

### Zou Zou’s Mediterranean dinner near Manhattan West

More restaurants — Zou Zou's

Official menu lists a shareable dips section and raw-bar items, making it useful for a group dinner or lighter grazing near Penn Station/Hudson Yards.

Price: dips: any three for $29 or all five for $39; dressed Big Rock oysters $5 each; Chef’s Menu $79 per person for parties up to 8  
Schedule: Official metadata says open daily for dine-in dinner; exact dinner hours not in fetched text  
Conditions: Ordinary dinner/restaurant card unless rewritten around the $79 Chef’s Menu. Not a happy hour or discount; tax/tip/service charges not stated. Source: https://www.zouzousnyc.com/menus/.  
[Source](https://www.zouzousnyc.com/menus/)

### Quality Bistro Midtown brasserie and steakhouse

More restaurants — Quality Bistro

Official menu and address were current in fetched structured data; useful for Midtown steakhouse/brasserie dining with a broad cocktail and dinner menu.

Price: examples from fetched dinner menu: Bistro Spritz $21, Lillet Cup $20, Spicy Bird $24  
Schedule: Dinner schedule not stated in fetched menu text  
Conditions: Ordinary restaurant menu; no verified discount, prix-fixe, or finite special in captured menu text. Tax/tip/service charges not stated. Source: https://www.qualitybistro.com/menus/.  
[Source](https://www.qualitybistro.com/menus/)

### The Fulton waterfront seafood at Pier 17

More restaurants — The Fulton

Official menu lists waterside dining at Pier 17 with seafood plates, raw bar, and simple mains, useful for a view-oriented downtown seafood dinner.

Price: examples: half-dozen oysters $26, petite seafood plateau $38, black sea bass $40, prime NY strip $65  
Schedule: Dinner menu checked; exact hours not stated in fetched text  
Conditions: Ordinary restaurant menu, not a discount; dinner/lunch/brunch menu sections were captured but exact operating hours were not stated in menu text. Tax/tip/service charges not stated. Source: https://www.thefulton.nyc/menus/.  
[Source](https://www.thefulton.nyc/menus/)

### Ambassadors Clubhouse Punjabi restaurant in NoMad

More restaurants — Ambassadors Clubhouse

Eater describes the London group's NYC restaurant as an 8,000-square-foot, two-story space with papads, chaats, chutneys, kebabs, biryani, matka, and karahi dishes under chef Karan Mittal.

Conditions: Do not present as a September-new opening: Eater says it opened in February and remained on the heatmap. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap.  
Published: 2026-09-03  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap)

_No API key is needed for these public websites. `not checked` is distinct from a checked source with no final pick._

- **[arts] Anthology Film Archives — used:** Official calendar exposed exact Sept./Oct. showtimes; retained 11 film events.
- **[arts] Atlantic Theater Company — blocked (fetch failed):** Configured what's-on URL returned HTTP 404.
- **[arts] Balance — used:** largest source: Anthology Film Archives 11/37; Brooklyn 8/37. Brooklyn share missed the 40% target because Brooklyn Museum was rate-limited, Nitehawk and BAM calendars did not expose dated listings, and only Target Margin Theater yielded verified Brooklyn arts-lane events.
